cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(matmul_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(matmul matmul.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(matmul PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(matmul m)
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];

            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;

                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];

                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);

            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t vectorWidth = 8;
+constexpr size_t maxPanelWidth = 256;
+
+size_t ceilDivide(size_t value, size_t divisor) {
+    return value / divisor + (value % divisor != 0);
+}
+
+// Balanced contiguous partitions, including empty blocks when N is small.
+size_t blockSize(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return N / parts + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < N % parts);
+}
+
+size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return (N / parts) * coordinate +
+           std::min(N % parts, static_cast<size_t>(coordinate));
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    MPI_Comm cart = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm row = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm column = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+    int rank = 0;
+    int processes = 0;
+
+    ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processes);
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
+        int periodic[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dimensions, periodic, 0, &cart);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(cart, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(cart, rank, 2, coordinates);
+        int keepRow[2] = {0, 1};
+        int keepColumn[2] = {1, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_sub(cart, keepRow, &row);
+        MPI_Cart_sub(cart, keepColumn, &column);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&row);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&column);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&cart);
+    }
+};
+
+// Each rank holds one C rectangle and cyclically owned A/B panels. B is
+// packed into eight-column strips so the inner kernel streams through L1.
+struct LocalMatrices {
+    size_t rows;
+    size_t columns;
+    size_t firstRow;
+    size_t firstColumn;
+    size_t stride;
+    size_t panelWidth;
+    size_t panels;
+    std::vector<double> A;
+    std::vector<double> B;
+    std::vector<double> C;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 2> receiveA;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 2> receiveB;
+
+    LocalMatrices(size_t N, const ProcessGrid& grid)
+        : rows(blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[0], grid.coordinates[0])),
+          columns(blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[1], grid.coordinates[1])),
+          firstRow(blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[0], grid.coordinates[0])),
+          firstColumn(blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[1], grid.coordinates[1])),
+          stride(ceilDivide(columns, vectorWidth) * vectorWidth),
+          panelWidth(1), panels(0) {
+        if (N != 0) {
+            // Enough panels to balance their ownership, and MPI counts that
+            // remain representable even for matrices larger than INT_MAX.
+            const size_t largestBlock = ceilDivide(N, std::min(grid.dimensions[0],
+                                                              grid.dimensions[1]));
+            const size_t largestStride = ceilDivide(largestBlock, vectorWidth) * vectorWidth;
+            panelWidth = std::min({maxPanelWidth,
+                                  ceilDivide(N, std::max(grid.dimensions[0], grid.dimensions[1])),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / largestStride});
+            panels = ceilDivide(N, panelWidth);
+        }
+
+        size_t ownedColumns = 0;
+        size_t ownedRows = 0;
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panels; ++panel) {
+            const size_t width = std::min(panelWidth, N - panel * panelWidth);
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[1] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[1])) {
+                ownedColumns += width;
+            }
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[0] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[0])) {
+                ownedRows += width;
+            }
+        }
+        A.resize(rows * ownedColumns);
+        B.resize(stride * ownedRows);
+        C.resize(rows * stride);
+        for (size_t slot = 0; slot < 2; ++slot) {
+            receiveA[slot].resize(rows * panelWidth);
+            receiveB[slot].resize(stride * panelWidth);
+        }
+
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panels; ++panel) {
+            const size_t firstK = panel * panelWidth;
+            const size_t width = std::min(panelWidth, N - firstK);
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[1] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[1])) {
+                double* values = A.data() + (panel / grid.dimensions[1]) * panelWidth * rows;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+                        values[i * width + k] = getPseudoRndValue(N, firstRow + i, firstK + k);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[0] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[0])) {
+                double* values = B.data() + (panel / grid.dimensions[0]) * panelWidth * stride;
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; j += vectorWidth) {
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < std::min(vectorWidth, columns - j); ++lane) {
+                            values[j * width + k * vectorWidth + lane] =
+                                getPseudoRndValue(N, firstK + k, firstColumn + j + lane);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+// Independent columns vectorize without reassociating the k reduction.
+// A 4x8 microtile provides independent accumulators and reuses each B load.
+template <size_t Rows>
+void multiplyRows(const double* A, const double* B, double* C,
+                  size_t width, size_t stride) {
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < stride; j += vectorWidth) {
+        alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorWidth];
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                sums[i][lane] = C[i * stride + j + lane];
+            }
+        }
+        const double* packedB = B + j * width;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+                const double a = A[i * width + k];
+                for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                    sums[i][lane] += a * packedB[k * vectorWidth + lane];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                C[i * stride + j + lane] = sums[i][lane];
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {

-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void multiplyPanel(const double* A, const double* B, LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t width) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 4 <= matrices.rows; i += 4) {
+        multiplyRows<4>(A + i * width, B, matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride,
+                        width, matrices.stride);
+    }
+    for (; i < matrices.rows; ++i) {
+        multiplyRows<1>(A + i * width, B, matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride,
+                        width, matrices.stride);
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    if (matrices.panels == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    struct PendingPanel {
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        size_t width = 0;
+        MPI_Request requests[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};
+    } pending[2];
+
+    auto postPanel = [&](size_t panel, size_t slot) {
+        auto& current = pending[slot];
+        current.width = std::min(matrices.panelWidth, N - panel * matrices.panelWidth);
+        const int ownerA = static_cast<int>(panel % grid.dimensions[1]);
+        const int ownerB = static_cast<int>(panel % grid.dimensions[0]);
+        current.A = matrices.receiveA[slot].data();
+        current.B = matrices.receiveB[slot].data();
+        // Broadcast directly from owned panels, avoiding packing/copying in
+        // the timed section. Subcommunicator ranks equal their coordinates.
+        if (ownerA == grid.coordinates[1] && matrices.rows != 0) {
+            current.A = matrices.A.data() +
+                        (panel / grid.dimensions[1]) * matrices.panelWidth * matrices.rows;
+        }
+        if (ownerB == grid.coordinates[0] && matrices.stride != 0) {
+            current.B = matrices.B.data() +
+                        (panel / grid.dimensions[0]) * matrices.panelWidth * matrices.stride;
+        }
+        MPI_Ibcast(current.A, static_cast<int>(matrices.rows * current.width),
+                   MPI_DOUBLE, ownerA, grid.row, &current.requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(current.B, static_cast<int>(matrices.stride * current.width),
+                   MPI_DOUBLE, ownerB, grid.column, &current.requests[1]);
+    };
+
+    postPanel(0, 0);
+    for (size_t panel = 0; panel < matrices.panels; ++panel) {
+        auto& current = pending[panel % 2];
+        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        if (panel + 1 < matrices.panels) {
+            postPanel(panel + 1, (panel + 1) % 2);
+        }
+        if (matrices.rows != 0 && matrices.columns != 0) {
+            multiplyPanel(current.A, current.B, matrices, current.width);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Only -r requires a full matrix. Derived datatypes place each rectangle
+// directly into row-major C and avoid both int-sized element counts and a
+// second root-side receive buffer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N,
+                                 const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(N * N);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < matrices.rows; ++i) {
+            std::copy_n(matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride, matrices.columns,
+                        result.data() + (matrices.firstRow + i) * N + matrices.firstColumn);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < grid.processes; ++source) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.cart, source, 2, coordinates);
+            const size_t rows = blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[0], coordinates[0]);
+            const size_t columns = blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[1], coordinates[1]);
+            if (rows == 0 || columns == 0) {
+                continue;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            MPI_Datatype rectangle;
+            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
+                            static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &rectangle);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&rectangle);
+            const size_t offset = blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[0], coordinates[0]) * N +
+                                  blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[1], coordinates[1]);
+            MPI_Recv(result.data() + offset, 1, rectangle, source, 0, grid.cart, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            MPI_Type_free(&rectangle);
         }
+    } else if (matrices.rows != 0 && matrices.columns != 0) {
+        MPI_Datatype rectangle;
+        MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(matrices.rows), static_cast<int>(matrices.columns),
+                        static_cast<int>(matrices.stride), MPI_DOUBLE, &rectangle);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&rectangle);
+        MPI_Send(matrices.C.data(), 1, rectangle, 0, 0, grid.cart);
+        MPI_Type_free(&rectangle);
     }
+    return result;
 }

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t vectorWidth = 8;
+constexpr size_t maxPanelWidth = 256;
+
+size_t ceilDivide(size_t value, size_t divisor) {
+    return value / divisor + (value % divisor != 0);
+}
+
+// Balanced contiguous partitions, including empty blocks when N is small.
+size_t blockSize(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return N / parts + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < N % parts);
+}
+
+size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return (N / parts) * coordinate +
+           std::min(N % parts, static_cast<size_t>(coordinate));
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    MPI_Comm cart = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm row = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm column = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+    int rank = 0;
+    int processes = 0;
+
+    ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processes);
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
+        int periodic[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dimensions, periodic, 0, &cart);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(cart, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(cart, rank, 2, coordinates);
+        int keepRow[2] = {0, 1};
+        int keepColumn[2] = {1, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_sub(cart, keepRow, &row);
+        MPI_Cart_sub(cart, keepColumn, &column);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&row);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&column);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&cart);
+    }
+};
+
+// Each rank holds one C rectangle and cyclically owned A/B panels. B is
+// packed into eight-column strips so the inner kernel streams through L1.
+struct LocalMatrices {
+    size_t rows;
+    size_t columns;
+    size_t firstRow;
+    size_t firstColumn;
+    size_t stride;
+    size_t panelWidth;
+    size_t panels;
+    std::vector<double> A;
+    std::vector<double> B;
+    std::vector<double> C;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 2> receiveA;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 2> receiveB;
+
+    LocalMatrices(size_t N, const ProcessGrid& grid)
+        : rows(blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[0], grid.coordinates[0])),
+          columns(blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[1], grid.coordinates[1])),
+          firstRow(blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[0], grid.coordinates[0])),
+          firstColumn(blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[1], grid.coordinates[1])),
+          stride(ceilDivide(columns, vectorWidth) * vectorWidth),
+          panelWidth(1), panels(0) {
+        if (N != 0) {
+            // Enough panels to balance their ownership, and MPI counts that
+            // remain representable even for matrices larger than INT_MAX.
+            const size_t largestBlock = ceilDivide(N, std::min(grid.dimensions[0],
+                                                              grid.dimensions[1]));
+            const size_t largestStride = ceilDivide(largestBlock, vectorWidth) * vectorWidth;
+            panelWidth = std::min({maxPanelWidth,
+                                  ceilDivide(N, std::max(grid.dimensions[0], grid.dimensions[1])),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / largestStride});
+            panels = ceilDivide(N, panelWidth);
+        }
+
+        size_t ownedColumns = 0;
+        size_t ownedRows = 0;
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panels; ++panel) {
+            const size_t width = std::min(panelWidth, N - panel * panelWidth);
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[1] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[1])) {
+                ownedColumns += width;
+            }
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[0] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[0])) {
+                ownedRows += width;
+            }
+        }
+        A.resize(rows * ownedColumns);
+        B.resize(stride * ownedRows);
+        C.resize(rows * stride);
+        for (size_t slot = 0; slot < 2; ++slot) {
+            receiveA[slot].resize(rows * panelWidth);
+            receiveB[slot].resize(stride * panelWidth);
+        }
+
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panels; ++panel) {
+            const size_t firstK = panel * panelWidth;
+            const size_t width = std::min(panelWidth, N - firstK);
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[1] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[1])) {
+                double* values = A.data() + (panel / grid.dimensions[1]) * panelWidth * rows;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+                        values[i * width + k] = getPseudoRndValue(N, firstRow + i, firstK + k);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[0] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[0])) {
+                double* values = B.data() + (panel / grid.dimensions[0]) * panelWidth * stride;
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; j += vectorWidth) {
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < std::min(vectorWidth, columns - j); ++lane) {
+                            values[j * width + k * vectorWidth + lane] =
+                                getPseudoRndValue(N, firstK + k, firstColumn + j + lane);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+// Independent columns vectorize without reassociating the k reduction.
+// A 4x8 microtile provides independent accumulators and reuses each B load.
+template <size_t Rows>
+void multiplyRows(const double* A, const double* B, double* C,
+                  size_t width, size_t stride) {
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < stride; j += vectorWidth) {
+        alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorWidth];
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                sums[i][lane] = C[i * stride + j + lane];
+            }
+        }
+        const double* packedB = B + j * width;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+                const double a = A[i * width + k];
+                for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                    sums[i][lane] += a * packedB[k * vectorWidth + lane];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                C[i * stride + j + lane] = sums[i][lane];
+            }
         }
     }
 }

-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void multiplyPanel(const double* A, const double* B, LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t width) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 4 <= matrices.rows; i += 4) {
+        multiplyRows<4>(A + i * width, B, matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride,
+                        width, matrices.stride);
+    }
+    for (; i < matrices.rows; ++i) {
+        multiplyRows<1>(A + i * width, B, matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride,
+                        width, matrices.stride);
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    if (matrices.panels == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    struct PendingPanel {
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        size_t width = 0;
+        MPI_Request requests[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};
+    } pending[2];
+
+    auto postPanel = [&](size_t panel, size_t slot) {
+        auto& current = pending[slot];
+        current.width = std::min(matrices.panelWidth, N - panel * matrices.panelWidth);
+        const int ownerA = static_cast<int>(panel % grid.dimensions[1]);
+        const int ownerB = static_cast<int>(panel % grid.dimensions[0]);
+        current.A = matrices.receiveA[slot].data();
+        current.B = matrices.receiveB[slot].data();
+        // Broadcast directly from owned panels, avoiding packing/copying in
+        // the timed section. Subcommunicator ranks equal their coordinates.
+        if (ownerA == grid.coordinates[1] && matrices.rows != 0) {
+            current.A = matrices.A.data() +
+                        (panel / grid.dimensions[1]) * matrices.panelWidth * matrices.rows;
+        }
+        if (ownerB == grid.coordinates[0] && matrices.stride != 0) {
+            current.B = matrices.B.data() +
+                        (panel / grid.dimensions[0]) * matrices.panelWidth * matrices.stride;
+        }
+        MPI_Ibcast(current.A, static_cast<int>(matrices.rows * current.width),
+                   MPI_DOUBLE, ownerA, grid.row, &current.requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(current.B, static_cast<int>(matrices.stride * current.width),
+                   MPI_DOUBLE, ownerB, grid.column, &current.requests[1]);
+    };
+
+    postPanel(0, 0);
+    for (size_t panel = 0; panel < matrices.panels; ++panel) {
+        auto& current = pending[panel % 2];
+        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        if (panel + 1 < matrices.panels) {
+            postPanel(panel + 1, (panel + 1) % 2);
+        }
+        if (matrices.rows != 0 && matrices.columns != 0) {
+            multiplyPanel(current.A, current.B, matrices, current.width);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Only -r requires a full matrix. Derived datatypes place each rectangle
+// directly into row-major C and avoid both int-sized element counts and a
+// second root-side receive buffer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N,
+                                 const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(N * N);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < matrices.rows; ++i) {
+            std::copy_n(matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride, matrices.columns,
+                        result.data() + (matrices.firstRow + i) * N + matrices.firstColumn);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < grid.processes; ++source) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.cart, source, 2, coordinates);
+            const size_t rows = blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[0], coordinates[0]);
+            const size_t columns = blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[1], coordinates[1]);
+            if (rows == 0 || columns == 0) {
+                continue;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            MPI_Datatype rectangle;
+            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
+                            static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &rectangle);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&rectangle);
+            const size_t offset = blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[0], coordinates[0]) * N +
+                                  blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[1], coordinates[1]);
+            MPI_Recv(result.data() + offset, 1, rectangle, source, 0, grid.cart, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            MPI_Type_free(&rectangle);
         }
+    } else if (matrices.rows != 0 && matrices.columns != 0) {
+        MPI_Datatype rectangle;
+        MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(matrices.rows), static_cast<int>(matrices.columns),
+                        static_cast<int>(matrices.stride), MPI_DOUBLE, &rectangle);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&rectangle);
+        MPI_Send(matrices.C.data(), 1, rectangle, 0, 0, grid.cart);
+        MPI_Type_free(&rectangle);
     }
+    return result;
 }

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t vectorWidth = 8;
+constexpr size_t maxPanelWidth = 256;
+
+size_t ceilDivide(size_t value, size_t divisor) {
+    return value / divisor + (value % divisor != 0);
+}
+
+// Balanced contiguous partitions, including empty blocks when N is small.
+size_t blockSize(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return N / parts + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < N % parts);
+}
+
+size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return (N / parts) * coordinate +
+           std::min(N % parts, static_cast<size_t>(coordinate));
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    MPI_Comm cart = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm row = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm column = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+    int rank = 0;
+    int processes = 0;
+
+    ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processes);
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
+        int periodic[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dimensions, periodic, 0, &cart);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(cart, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(cart, rank, 2, coordinates);
+        int keepRow[2] = {0, 1};
+        int keepColumn[2] = {1, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_sub(cart, keepRow, &row);
+        MPI_Cart_sub(cart, keepColumn, &column);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&row);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&column);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&cart);
+    }
+};
+
+// Each rank holds one C rectangle and cyclically owned A/B panels. B is
+// packed into eight-column strips so the inner kernel streams through L1.
+struct LocalMatrices {
+    size_t rows;
+    size_t columns;
+    size_t firstRow;
+    size_t firstColumn;
+    size_t stride;
+    size_t panelWidth;
+    size_t panels;
+    std::vector<double> A;
+    std::vector<double> B;
+    std::vector<double> C;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 2> receiveA;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 2> receiveB;
+
+    LocalMatrices(size_t N, const ProcessGrid& grid)
+        : rows(blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[0], grid.coordinates[0])),
+          columns(blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[1], grid.coordinates[1])),
+          firstRow(blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[0], grid.coordinates[0])),
+          firstColumn(blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[1], grid.coordinates[1])),
+          stride(ceilDivide(columns, vectorWidth) * vectorWidth),
+          panelWidth(1), panels(0) {
+        if (N != 0) {
+            // Enough panels to balance their ownership, and MPI counts that
+            // remain representable even for matrices larger than INT_MAX.
+            const size_t largestBlock = ceilDivide(N, std::min(grid.dimensions[0],
+                                                              grid.dimensions[1]));
+            const size_t largestStride = ceilDivide(largestBlock, vectorWidth) * vectorWidth;
+            panelWidth = std::min({maxPanelWidth,
+                                  ceilDivide(N, std::max(grid.dimensions[0], grid.dimensions[1])),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / largestStride});
+            panels = ceilDivide(N, panelWidth);
+        }
+
+        size_t ownedColumns = 0;
+        size_t ownedRows = 0;
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panels; ++panel) {
+            const size_t width = std::min(panelWidth, N - panel * panelWidth);
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[1] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[1])) {
+                ownedColumns += width;
+            }
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[0] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[0])) {
+                ownedRows += width;
+            }
+        }
+        A.resize(rows * ownedColumns);
+        B.resize(stride * ownedRows);
+        C.resize(rows * stride);
+        for (size_t slot = 0; slot < 2; ++slot) {
+            receiveA[slot].resize(rows * panelWidth);
+            receiveB[slot].resize(stride * panelWidth);
+        }
+
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panels; ++panel) {
+            const size_t firstK = panel * panelWidth;
+            const size_t width = std::min(panelWidth, N - firstK);
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[1] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[1])) {
+                double* values = A.data() + (panel / grid.dimensions[1]) * panelWidth * rows;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+                        values[i * width + k] = getPseudoRndValue(N, firstRow + i, firstK + k);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[0] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[0])) {
+                double* values = B.data() + (panel / grid.dimensions[0]) * panelWidth * stride;
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; j += vectorWidth) {
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < std::min(vectorWidth, columns - j); ++lane) {
+                            values[j * width + k * vectorWidth + lane] =
+                                getPseudoRndValue(N, firstK + k, firstColumn + j + lane);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+// Independent columns vectorize without reassociating the k reduction.
+// A 4x8 microtile provides independent accumulators and reuses each B load.
+template <size_t Rows>
+void multiplyRows(const double* A, const double* B, double* C,
+                  size_t width, size_t stride) {
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < stride; j += vectorWidth) {
+        alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorWidth];
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                sums[i][lane] = C[i * stride + j + lane];
+            }
+        }
+        const double* packedB = B + j * width;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+                const double a = A[i * width + k];
+                for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                    sums[i][lane] += a * packedB[k * vectorWidth + lane];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                C[i * stride + j + lane] = sums[i][lane];
+            }
         }
     }
 }

-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void multiplyPanel(const double* A, const double* B, LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t width) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 4 <= matrices.rows; i += 4) {
+        multiplyRows<4>(A + i * width, B, matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride,
+                        width, matrices.stride);
+    }
+    for (; i < matrices.rows; ++i) {
+        multiplyRows<1>(A + i * width, B, matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride,
+                        width, matrices.stride);
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    if (matrices.panels == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    struct PendingPanel {
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        size_t width = 0;
+        MPI_Request requests[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};
+    } pending[2];
+
+    auto postPanel = [&](size_t panel, size_t slot) {
+        auto& current = pending[slot];
+        current.width = std::min(matrices.panelWidth, N - panel * matrices.panelWidth);
+        const int ownerA = static_cast<int>(panel % grid.dimensions[1]);
+        const int ownerB = static_cast<int>(panel % grid.dimensions[0]);
+        current.A = matrices.receiveA[slot].data();
+        current.B = matrices.receiveB[slot].data();
+        // Broadcast directly from owned panels, avoiding packing/copying in
+        // the timed section. Subcommunicator ranks equal their coordinates.
+        if (ownerA == grid.coordinates[1] && matrices.rows != 0) {
+            current.A = matrices.A.data() +
+                        (panel / grid.dimensions[1]) * matrices.panelWidth * matrices.rows;
+        }
+        if (ownerB == grid.coordinates[0] && matrices.stride != 0) {
+            current.B = matrices.B.data() +
+                        (panel / grid.dimensions[0]) * matrices.panelWidth * matrices.stride;
+        }
+        MPI_Ibcast(current.A, static_cast<int>(matrices.rows * current.width),
+                   MPI_DOUBLE, ownerA, grid.row, &current.requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(current.B, static_cast<int>(matrices.stride * current.width),
+                   MPI_DOUBLE, ownerB, grid.column, &current.requests[1]);
+    };
+
+    postPanel(0, 0);
+    for (size_t panel = 0; panel < matrices.panels; ++panel) {
+        auto& current = pending[panel % 2];
+        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        if (panel + 1 < matrices.panels) {
+            postPanel(panel + 1, (panel + 1) % 2);
+        }
+        if (matrices.rows != 0 && matrices.columns != 0) {
+            multiplyPanel(current.A, current.B, matrices, current.width);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Only -r requires a full matrix. Derived datatypes place each rectangle
+// directly into row-major C and avoid both int-sized element counts and a
+// second root-side receive buffer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N,
+                                 const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(N * N);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < matrices.rows; ++i) {
+            std::copy_n(matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride, matrices.columns,
+                        result.data() + (matrices.firstRow + i) * N + matrices.firstColumn);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < grid.processes; ++source) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.cart, source, 2, coordinates);
+            const size_t rows = blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[0], coordinates[0]);
+            const size_t columns = blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[1], coordinates[1]);
+            if (rows == 0 || columns == 0) {
+                continue;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            MPI_Datatype rectangle;
+            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
+                            static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &rectangle);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&rectangle);
+            const size_t offset = blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[0], coordinates[0]) * N +
+                                  blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[1], coordinates[1]);
+            MPI_Recv(result.data() + offset, 1, rectangle, source, 0, grid.cart, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            MPI_Type_free(&rectangle);
         }
+    } else if (matrices.rows != 0 && matrices.columns != 0) {
+        MPI_Datatype rectangle;
+        MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(matrices.rows), static_cast<int>(matrices.columns),
+                        static_cast<int>(matrices.stride), MPI_DOUBLE, &rectangle);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&rectangle);
+        MPI_Send(matrices.C.data(), 1, rectangle, 0, 0, grid.cart);
+        MPI_Type_free(&rectangle);
     }
+    return result;
 }

#include <mpi.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cerrno>
#include <climits>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <exception>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

namespace {

constexpr size_t vectorWidth = 8;
constexpr size_t maxPanelWidth = 256;

size_t ceilDivide(size_t value, size_t divisor) {
    return value / divisor + (value % divisor != 0);
}

// Balanced contiguous partitions, including empty blocks when N is small.
size_t blockSize(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
    return N / parts + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < N % parts);
}

size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
    return (N / parts) * coordinate +
           std::min(N % parts, static_cast<size_t>(coordinate));
}

struct ProcessGrid {
    MPI_Comm cart = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    MPI_Comm row = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    MPI_Comm column = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
    int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
    int rank = 0;
    int processes = 0;

    ProcessGrid() {
        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processes);
        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
        int periodic[2] = {0, 0};
        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dimensions, periodic, 0, &cart);
        MPI_Comm_rank(cart, &rank);
        MPI_Cart_coords(cart, rank, 2, coordinates);
        int keepRow[2] = {0, 1};
        int keepColumn[2] = {1, 0};
        MPI_Cart_sub(cart, keepRow, &row);
        MPI_Cart_sub(cart, keepColumn, &column);
    }

    ~ProcessGrid() {
        MPI_Comm_free(&row);
        MPI_Comm_free(&column);
        MPI_Comm_free(&cart);
    }
};

// Each rank holds one C rectangle and cyclically owned A/B panels. B is
// packed into eight-column strips so the inner kernel streams through L1.
struct LocalMatrices {
    size_t rows;
    size_t columns;
    size_t firstRow;
    size_t firstColumn;
    size_t stride;
    size_t panelWidth;
    size_t panels;
    std::vector<double> A;
    std::vector<double> B;
    std::vector<double> C;
    std::array<std::vector<double>, 2> receiveA;
    std::array<std::vector<double>, 2> receiveB;

    LocalMatrices(size_t N, const ProcessGrid& grid)
        : rows(blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[0], grid.coordinates[0])),
          columns(blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[1], grid.coordinates[1])),
          firstRow(blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[0], grid.coordinates[0])),
          firstColumn(blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[1], grid.coordinates[1])),
          stride(ceilDivide(columns, vectorWidth) * vectorWidth),
          panelWidth(1), panels(0) {
        if (N != 0) {
            // Enough panels to balance their ownership, and MPI counts that
            // remain representable even for matrices larger than INT_MAX.
            const size_t largestBlock = ceilDivide(N, std::min(grid.dimensions[0],
                                                              grid.dimensions[1]));
            const size_t largestStride = ceilDivide(largestBlock, vectorWidth) * vectorWidth;
            panelWidth = std::min({maxPanelWidth,
                                  ceilDivide(N, std::max(grid.dimensions[0], grid.dimensions[1])),
                                  static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / largestStride});
            panels = ceilDivide(N, panelWidth);
        }

        size_t ownedColumns = 0;
        size_t ownedRows = 0;
        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panels; ++panel) {
            const size_t width = std::min(panelWidth, N - panel * panelWidth);
            if (panel % grid.dimensions[1] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[1])) {
                ownedColumns += width;
            }
            if (panel % grid.dimensions[0] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[0])) {
                ownedRows += width;
            }
        }
        A.resize(rows * ownedColumns);
        B.resize(stride * ownedRows);
        C.resize(rows * stride);
        for (size_t slot = 0; slot < 2; ++slot) {
            receiveA[slot].resize(rows * panelWidth);
            receiveB[slot].resize(stride * panelWidth);
        }

        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panels; ++panel) {
            const size_t firstK = panel * panelWidth;
            const size_t width = std::min(panelWidth, N - firstK);
            if (panel % grid.dimensions[1] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[1])) {
                double* values = A.data() + (panel / grid.dimensions[1]) * panelWidth * rows;
                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
                    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
                        values[i * width + k] = getPseudoRndValue(N, firstRow + i, firstK + k);
                    }
                }
            }
            if (panel % grid.dimensions[0] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[0])) {
                double* values = B.data() + (panel / grid.dimensions[0]) * panelWidth * stride;
                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; j += vectorWidth) {
                    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < std::min(vectorWidth, columns - j); ++lane) {
                            values[j * width + k * vectorWidth + lane] =
                                getPseudoRndValue(N, firstK + k, firstColumn + j + lane);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
};

// Independent columns vectorize without reassociating the k reduction.
// A 4x8 microtile provides independent accumulators and reuses each B load.
template <size_t Rows>
void multiplyRows(const double* A, const double* B, double* C,
                  size_t width, size_t stride) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < stride; j += vectorWidth) {
        alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorWidth];
        for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
                sums[i][lane] = C[i * stride + j + lane];
            }
        }
        const double* packedB = B + j * width;
        for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
                const double a = A[i * width + k];
                for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
                    sums[i][lane] += a * packedB[k * vectorWidth + lane];
                }
            }
        }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
                C[i * stride + j + lane] = sums[i][lane];
            }
        }
    }
}

void multiplyPanel(const double* A, const double* B, LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t width) {
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 4 <= matrices.rows; i += 4) {
        multiplyRows<4>(A + i * width, B, matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride,
                        width, matrices.stride);
    }
    for (; i < matrices.rows; ++i) {
        multiplyRows<1>(A + i * width, B, matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride,
                        width, matrices.stride);
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
    if (matrices.panels == 0) {
        return;
    }
    struct PendingPanel {
        double* A = nullptr;
        double* B = nullptr;
        size_t width = 0;
        MPI_Request requests[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};
    } pending[2];

    auto postPanel = [&](size_t panel, size_t slot) {
        auto& current = pending[slot];
        current.width = std::min(matrices.panelWidth, N - panel * matrices.panelWidth);
        const int ownerA = static_cast<int>(panel % grid.dimensions[1]);
        const int ownerB = static_cast<int>(panel % grid.dimensions[0]);
        current.A = matrices.receiveA[slot].data();
        current.B = matrices.receiveB[slot].data();
        // Broadcast directly from owned panels, avoiding packing/copying in
        // the timed section. Subcommunicator ranks equal their coordinates.
        if (ownerA == grid.coordinates[1] && matrices.rows != 0) {
            current.A = matrices.A.data() +
                        (panel / grid.dimensions[1]) * matrices.panelWidth * matrices.rows;
        }
        if (ownerB == grid.coordinates[0] && matrices.stride != 0) {
            current.B = matrices.B.data() +
                        (panel / grid.dimensions[0]) * matrices.panelWidth * matrices.stride;
        }
        MPI_Ibcast(current.A, static_cast<int>(matrices.rows * current.width),
                   MPI_DOUBLE, ownerA, grid.row, &current.requests[0]);
        MPI_Ibcast(current.B, static_cast<int>(matrices.stride * current.width),
                   MPI_DOUBLE, ownerB, grid.column, &current.requests[1]);
    };

    postPanel(0, 0);
    for (size_t panel = 0; panel < matrices.panels; ++panel) {
        auto& current = pending[panel % 2];
        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
        if (panel + 1 < matrices.panels) {
            postPanel(panel + 1, (panel + 1) % 2);
        }
        if (matrices.rows != 0 && matrices.columns != 0) {
            multiplyPanel(current.A, current.B, matrices, current.width);
        }
    }
}

// Only -r requires a full matrix. Derived datatypes place each rectangle
// directly into row-major C and avoid both int-sized element counts and a
// second root-side receive buffer.
std::vector<double> gatherResult(const LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N,
                                 const ProcessGrid& grid) {
    std::vector<double> result;
    if (grid.rank == 0) {
        result.resize(N * N);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < matrices.rows; ++i) {
            std::copy_n(matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride, matrices.columns,
                        result.data() + (matrices.firstRow + i) * N + matrices.firstColumn);
        }
        for (int source = 1; source < grid.processes; ++source) {
            int coordinates[2];
            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.cart, source, 2, coordinates);
            const size_t rows = blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[0], coordinates[0]);
            const size_t columns = blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[1], coordinates[1]);
            if (rows == 0 || columns == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            MPI_Datatype rectangle;
            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
                            static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &rectangle);

+// Only -r requires a full matrix. Derived datatypes place each rectangle

+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N,

+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t vectorWidth = 8;
+constexpr size_t maxPanelWidth = 256;
+
+size_t ceilDivide(size_t value, size_t divisor) {
+    return value / divisor + (value % divisor != 0);
+}
+
+// Balanced contiguous partitions, including empty blocks when N is small.
+size_t blockSize(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return N / parts + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < N % parts);
+}
+
+size_t blockStart(size_t N, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    return (N / parts) * coordinate +
+           std::min(N % parts, static_cast<size_t>(coordinate));
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    MPI_Comm cart = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm row = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm column = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+    int rank = 0;
+    int processes = 0;
+
+    ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processes);
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
+        int periodic[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dimensions, periodic, 0, &cart);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(cart, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(cart, rank, 2, coordinates);
+        int keepRow[2] = {0, 1};
+        int keepColumn[2] = {1, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_sub(cart, keepRow, &row);
+        MPI_Cart_sub(cart, keepColumn, &column);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&row);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&column);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&cart);
+    }
+};
+
+// Each rank holds one C rectangle and cyclically owned A/B panels. B is
+// packed into eight-column strips so the inner kernel streams through L1.
+struct LocalMatrices {
+    size_t rows;
+    size_t columns;
+    size_t firstRow;
+    size_t firstColumn;
+    size_t stride;
+    size_t panelWidth;
+    size_t panels;
+    std::vector<double> A;
+    std::vector<double> B;
+    std::vector<double> C;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 2> receiveA;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 2> receiveB;
+
+    LocalMatrices(size_t N, const ProcessGrid& grid)
+        : rows(blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[0], grid.coordinates[0])),
+          columns(blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[1], grid.coordinates[1])),
+          firstRow(blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[0], grid.coordinates[0])),
+          firstColumn(blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[1], grid.coordinates[1])),
+          stride(ceilDivide(columns, vectorWidth) * vectorWidth),
+          panelWidth(1), panels(0) {
+        if (N != 0) {
+            // Enough panels to balance their ownership, and MPI counts that
+            // remain representable even for matrices larger than INT_MAX.
+            const size_t largestBlock = ceilDivide(N, std::min(grid.dimensions[0],
+                                                              grid.dimensions[1]));
+            const size_t largestStride = ceilDivide(largestBlock, vectorWidth) * vectorWidth;
+            panelWidth = std::min({maxPanelWidth,
+                                  ceilDivide(N, std::max(grid.dimensions[0], grid.dimensions[1])),
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / largestStride});
+            panels = ceilDivide(N, panelWidth);
+        }
+
+        size_t ownedColumns = 0;
+        size_t ownedRows = 0;
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panels; ++panel) {
+            const size_t width = std::min(panelWidth, N - panel * panelWidth);
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[1] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[1])) {
+                ownedColumns += width;
+            }
+            if (panel % grid.dimensions[0] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[0])) {
+                ownedRows += width;
+            }
+        }
+        A.resize(rows * ownedColumns);
+        B.resize(stride * ownedRows);
+        C.resize(rows * stride);
+        for (size_t slot = 0; slot < 2; ++slot) {
+            receiveA[slot].resize(rows * panelWidth);
+            receiveB[slot].resize(stride * panelWidth);
+        }
+
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panels; ++panel) {
+            const size_t firstK = panel * panelWidth;
+            const size_t width = std::min(panelWidth, N - firstK);
+            if (rows != 0 && panel % grid.dimensions[1] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[1])) {
+                double* values = A.data() + (panel / grid.dimensions[1]) * panelWidth * rows;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+                        values[i * width + k] = getPseudoRndValue(N, firstRow + i, firstK + k);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            if (stride != 0 && panel % grid.dimensions[0] == static_cast<size_t>(grid.coordinates[0])) {
+                double* values = B.data() + (panel / grid.dimensions[0]) * panelWidth * stride;
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; j += vectorWidth) {
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < std::min(vectorWidth, columns - j); ++lane) {
+                            values[j * width + k * vectorWidth + lane] =
+                                getPseudoRndValue(N, firstK + k, firstColumn + j + lane);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+// Independent columns vectorize without reassociating the k reduction.
+// A 4x8 microtile provides independent accumulators and reuses each B load.
+template <size_t Rows>
+void multiplyRows(const double* A, const double* B, double* C,
+                  size_t width, size_t stride) {
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < stride; j += vectorWidth) {
+        alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorWidth];
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                sums[i][lane] = C[i * stride + j + lane];
+            }
+        }
+        const double* packedB = B + j * width;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+                const double a = A[i * width + k];
+                for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                    sums[i][lane] += a * packedB[k * vectorWidth + lane];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < Rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorWidth; ++lane) {
+                C[i * stride + j + lane] = sums[i][lane];
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void multiplyPanel(const double* A, const double* B, LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t width) {
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + 4 <= matrices.rows; i += 4) {
+        multiplyRows<4>(A + i * width, B, matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride,
+                        width, matrices.stride);
+    }
+    for (; i < matrices.rows; ++i) {
+        multiplyRows<1>(A + i * width, B, matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride,
+                        width, matrices.stride);
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    if (matrices.panels == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    struct PendingPanel {
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        size_t width = 0;
+        MPI_Request requests[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};
+    } pending[2];
+
+    auto postPanel = [&](size_t panel, size_t slot) {
+        auto& current = pending[slot];
+        current.width = std::min(matrices.panelWidth, N - panel * matrices.panelWidth);
+        const int ownerA = static_cast<int>(panel % grid.dimensions[1]);
+        const int ownerB = static_cast<int>(panel % grid.dimensions[0]);
+        current.A = matrices.receiveA[slot].data();
+        current.B = matrices.receiveB[slot].data();
+        // Broadcast directly from owned panels, avoiding packing/copying in
+        // the timed section. Subcommunicator ranks equal their coordinates.
+        if (ownerA == grid.coordinates[1] && matrices.rows != 0) {
+            current.A = matrices.A.data() +
+                        (panel / grid.dimensions[1]) * matrices.panelWidth * matrices.rows;
+        }
+        if (ownerB == grid.coordinates[0] && matrices.stride != 0) {
+            current.B = matrices.B.data() +
+                        (panel / grid.dimensions[0]) * matrices.panelWidth * matrices.stride;
+        }
+        MPI_Ibcast(current.A, static_cast<int>(matrices.rows * current.width),
+                   MPI_DOUBLE, ownerA, grid.row, &current.requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(current.B, static_cast<int>(matrices.stride * current.width),
+                   MPI_DOUBLE, ownerB, grid.column, &current.requests[1]);
+    };
+
+    postPanel(0, 0);
+    for (size_t panel = 0; panel < matrices.panels; ++panel) {
+        auto& current = pending[panel % 2];
+        MPI_Waitall(2, current.requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        if (panel + 1 < matrices.panels) {
+            postPanel(panel + 1, (panel + 1) % 2);
+        }
+        if (matrices.rows != 0 && matrices.columns != 0) {
+            multiplyPanel(current.A, current.B, matrices, current.width);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Only -r requires a full matrix. Derived datatypes place each rectangle
+// directly into row-major C and avoid both int-sized element counts and a
+// second root-side receive buffer.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const LocalMatrices& matrices, size_t N,
+                                 const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(N * N);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < matrices.rows; ++i) {
+            std::copy_n(matrices.C.data() + i * matrices.stride, matrices.columns,
+                        result.data() + (matrices.firstRow + i) * N + matrices.firstColumn);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < grid.processes; ++source) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.cart, source, 2, coordinates);
+            const size_t rows = blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[0], coordinates[0]);
+            const size_t columns = blockSize(N, grid.dimensions[1], coordinates[1]);
+            if (rows == 0 || columns == 0) {
+                continue;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            MPI_Datatype rectangle;
+            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
+                            static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &rectangle);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&rectangle);
+            const size_t offset = blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[0], coordinates[0]) * N +
+                                  blockStart(N, grid.dimensions[1], coordinates[1]);
+            MPI_Recv(result.data() + offset, 1, rectangle, source, 0, grid.cart, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            MPI_Type_free(&rectangle);
         }
+    } else if (matrices.rows != 0 && matrices.columns != 0) {

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
